Fire Direction Officer

     1-99. The FDO supervises tactical and technical fire direction within the battalion. Based on guidance from
     the battalion commander, S-3, S-2, and targeting officer, the FDO decides where and how the battalion and
     any R units will fire. The FDO has specific responsibilities, which include:

          • Supervise the FDC.
          • Assist the FA battalion commander in developing tactical and technical fire direction guidance.
          • Develop and supervise input of appropriate parameters into the AFATDS, coordinating these

                parameters with maneuver battalion FSEs and the BCT FSE, R, reinforced FA units, and FAB HQ,
                as necessary.
          • Coordinate digital communications and database elements with users of non-FA digital C2 systems,
                as required.
          • Analyze requested targets for attack by FA. Consider ROE, desired effects, method of fire, and types
                of ammunition needed.
          • Verify dissemination of fire orders, schedules of fire, and schedules of targets to subordinate and
                supporting unit FDCs, FSEs, and the supported higher HQ FSE, as required.
          • Maintain the current target display or overlay.
          • Keep FA elements informed of targets.
          • Establish procedures for interface between primary automated systems and any other digital system
                with which the FDC may be required to interface.
          • Establish and maintain the tactical air integration system link to receive the airspace control overlay
                and facilitate coordination and clearance of airspace for mission execution.

Senior Fire Control Sergeant

     1-100. The senior fire control SGT is the automated technical and tactical fire control expert in the battalion
     FDC. The senior fire control SGT has specific responsibilities that may include:

          • Establish the tactical database in the battalion automated fire control system.
          • Establish procedures and train personnel to accomplish tactical and technical fire direction in a

                degraded (manual and voice) mode.
          • Monitor the technical input and executions of fire plans and missions.
          • Advise the FDO on changes or updates to battlefield geometry and firing unit status.
          • Crosscheck status boards with system data.
          • Assist the FDO in conducting assigned duties.
          • Responsible for the coordination and conduct of the FA technical rehearsal.
